Title: La Vie de Madame J. M. B. de La Mothe-Guyon, écrite par elle-même, qui contient toutes les expériences de la vie intérieure; nouvelle édition, tome III
Creator: Jeanne-Marie Bouvier de La Mothe-Guyon
Publisher: A Paris, chez les Libraires associés
Publication Date: 1791
Rights: Public Domain (published 1791, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT')
Language: fra
Source: Internet Archive item laviedemadamejm00guyogoog (https://archive.org/details/laviedemadamejm00guyogoog), the item's own laviedemadamejm00guyogoog_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: New York Public Library; host metadata date: 1791. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: c5856df67ec75ac53f9238f014f63812bddf26327406f7c969cc0bab4c7285f1.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; the host's front matter up to and including the line beginning 'des recherches en ligne dans le texte' removed (101 lines); nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. New York Public Library copy scanned by Google and contributed to the Internet Archive; good OCR, long s as f. The Google notice at the head (English and French) is removed by the strip marker.
Content note: The third and last volume of Guyon's Life in French, from her arrival in Paris and the renewed persecution of Father La Combe through the dealings with the bishop of Meaux and her imprisonments, with a chapter table at the front.
